(boxes shorter than the advance painted one twice). The fragmenters now record the line range of every fragment and the renderer paints
exactly that.

### F79 — right-to-left paragraphs over 64 KiB have a wrong text layer and only a coarse dropped-text check  (severity: low, size: medium — Parley)

What F70 could not fix: Parley's `u16` cluster offset (`ClusterData::text_offset`, `layout/data.rs:35`, set at `:897`) wraps in any run longer than 65 535 bytes. The fulgur fork
undoes the wrap for left-to-right text (clusters tile the text in order); for right-to-left runs the visual order is reversed and the same trick is not safe, so such a
paragraph keeps wrapped glyph text ranges (the text layer has the wrong characters for the tail) and the dropped-text check only asks whether the paragraph produced any glyph run.
The real fix is a wider offset in Parley (upstream bug, known, not reported; no fork exists).

### F80 — the lines of a bordered direct-text box end about 6 pt below the content box on every page  (severity: low, size: small — fulgur)

Found while adjusting `nested_paragraph_split.rs` for F78 (pre-existing, byte-identical before and after F78 on the first page):
`<div class="box">` with 400 `Line0001<br>…` lines, `.box { border: 1px solid; padding: 16px; line-height: 20px }`, A4, default
margins: 50 lines per page, the last one ends at 805.1 pt where the content box ends at 799.4 pt (input `out/scratch/f78/box20.html`, git-ignored;
with `line-height: normal`, `box.html`: 52 lines, 804.4 pt; the bare `<p>` is `p20.html`). The same lines as a bare `<p>` end at 792.4 pt. So the 17 px of border and padding
that `fragment_nested_inline_root` reserves above the lines (`usable = strips.fresh() - top_floor - inset_top - first_line`) are not
all subtracted when it fills a strip, or the renderer paints the lines lower than the fragment's `y + inset_top`; not investigated.
The F30 tests' 5 pt slack in `nested_paragraph_split.rs` hides it. Text stays inside the page box; it sits in the bottom margin.

### F81 — Blitz's `line-height: normal` is 1.2 em; Chrome uses the font's own metrics  (severity: low, size: small — Blitz)

Blitz `stylo_to_parley.rs:115` maps `normal` to 1.2. Chrome uses ascent + descent + line gap of the font (Inter's `hhea`: (1984 + 494) / 2048
= 1.21; not measured in Chrome here). Break positions of long unstyled text therefore differ slightly from the reference (a 16 px
line advances 19.2 px, Chrome about 19.4 px: about 1 line in 100). Found while planning F78, whose fix does not depend on it.
Fix by reading the primary font's metrics where `normal` is resolved; moves page breaks, so it needs fixtures and re-baselined goldens.

### F82 — one very long paragraph renders superlinearly slowly  (severity: low — not an invoice shape, size: unknown — Blitz / Parley / fulgur)

Measured for F78 (release CLI, before and after F78 alike): one `<p>` of 12 000 words 0.9 s (33 pages), 20 000 words 2.5 s (55 pages),
52 700 words 16 s (145 pages); 800 separate paragraphs of 60 words, 160 pages, 0.35 s. About n^1.8. Not profiled. AGENTS.md aims at 200
pages under a second; a document is not one paragraph, but a long legal text can be.

**Bench:** `bench/corpus/slow-paragraph.html` (B1; report only): one `<p>` of 40 000 words, 59 pages, 3.7 s on the dev VM (`docs/benchmarks.md`); `python3 scripts/bench.py slow-paragraph`. A fix shows up as a drop of that row.

### F84 — an inline image that starts a continuation page is drawn above the content box  (severity: low, size: small — fulgur)

From the `pdf-reviewer` run on PR #30 (pre-existing, identical on main): an inline `<img>` in the first line of a continuation page of a split paragraph has its
top at 40.7 pt where the content box starts at 42.5 pt (about 1.8 pt above; text is at the box). `recalculate_paragraph_line_boxes` (`convert/inline_root.rs:260`) keeps a
baseline model for such lines that differs from Parley's; the slice offset (F78) moves text and images by the same amount, so the gap is in the image's own `computed_y`.
Also L2 of the `vrt-validator` run: the image-to-text offset varies by up to 2.1 pt across the pages of such a paragraph (`/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f78/im/middle_1.3.html`).

### F85 — with `--tagged`, table cells are painted outside any marked-content sequence  (severity: medium, size: small — fulgur)

From the `pdf-reviewer` re-run on PR #31 (pre-existing, identical on main): fixture 12 with `--tagged` has 9 text-show operators outside any BDC, all in the items
table (header `POPIS`, `8 h`, `20 000,00`, …); fixture 01 has the same gap (26 of 57 text ops untagged on the first review). ISO 32000-1 §14.8.2.2 requires real content
to be in the structure tree or marked as an Artifact (PDF/UA-1 §7.1). Find which table-cell paint path skips `try_start_tagged`; add a tagged test that counts text-show
operators outside BDC on fixtures 01 and 12.

### F86 — text extraction of a dense one-page document stops after about 50 000 characters: poppler, not our PDF  (severity: was HIGH — **resolved** 2026-10-17, extractor limit)

Triage (2026-10-17, branch `f86-extraction-cap`, binary `target/release/htmltopdf` from main `36d4e5b`, `--css` + all `Inter-*.ttf`). Repros in `/tmp/claude-0/f86/`: `dense3_{6000,9000,12000}.pdf` (`font-size:3px;line-height:4px`), `dense1_{6000,9000,12000}.pdf` (`1px`/`2px`), `control_12000.pdf` (12 px, 15 pages); all one-page cases are `Pages: 1`.

- **Our PDF has every glyph.** `qpdf --qdf` plus a script (`/tmp/claude-0/f86-scripts/count_glyphs.py`) counts the 2-byte CIDs in every `TJ`/`Tj` operand and maps them through the ToUnicode CMap: `dense3_12000` has 83 999 glyphs = 72 000 non-space (= 6 x 12 000, exact) + 11 999 spaces, 0 unmapped, 12 000 distinct `wNNNNN` tokens, last one `w12000`. The 6 000 and 9 000 cases and the 1 px cases match exactly.
- **Second and third extractors read all 12 000 words**: pdfminer.six 20260107 and pypdf 6.21.1 (`/tmp/claude-0/f86-scripts/extract_compare.py`, libs in `/tmp/claude-0/f86-pylib`, run with `-I`) and `mutool draw -F txt` (1.23.10): 12 000 tokens, 0 missing, 0 extra, for every size.
- **Poppler (pdftotext 24.02.0) stops at 7 143 words** in `dense3_12000`, `dense1_12000` and `dense1_9000`, the same in default, `-raw`, `-layout` and `-bbox` modes; `dense3_9000` and `dense1_6000` are complete. The output is 50 003 bytes (42 858 non-space characters, last word `w07143` whole). This looks like a per-page character budget of about 50 000 in poppler's text output (inference from the byte count; poppler source not inspected).
- **Rasterised check** (`pdftoppm -r 600`): the bottom-right of `dense3_12000` shows `w11988` ... `w12000` drawn (`/tmp/claude-0/f86/crop_last_words_600dpi.png`).
- The original repros under `/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f30d/s/` no longer exist; the 8 334 count from the first report was not reproduced (here the cut is at 7 143 words for both sizes, consistent with a character budget, not a glyph or stream problem).
- Control: `control_12000` (15 pages) extracts all 12 000 words in every tool, including poppler.

Conclusion: the text is in the PDF and is extracted by three tools; the loss is poppler's per-page text output cap. Harmless for invoices. Not a fulgur item; no code change.

### F71 — a tab in `pre` / `white-space: pre` renders as a tofu box  (severity: medium, size: small — Blitz / fulgur)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #28 (pre-existing, `/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f66/atk/pre_tab.html`): Chrome expands a tab to the next
multiple of `tab-size` (8 spaces); the tab glyph is missing in Inter and Parley has no `tab-size`. Replace `\t` in preserved
text by the right number of spaces at the text-node push (`construct.rs`), counting from the line start.

Note from F70 (`pdf-reviewer` on PR #29): tabs in a bare `<pre>` are now reachable (before F70 a bare `<pre>` drew nothing at all), and a `.notdef` glyph
is a PDF/A blocker (ISO 19005-2 §6.2.11.7.2: every glyph used must be in the embedded font program, and `.notdef` must not be shown); see also F44.

### F72 — `::before` / `::after` content with a trailing space loses the space  (severity: low, size: small — Blitz) — **done** (PR #53, via F154: `content: "Pred "` keeps its space)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #28 (pre-existing, `/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f66/atk/ps.html`).

### F73 — `table { width: 100% }` with percentage cell widths overflows its column in a multi-column container  (severity: low, size: medium — Blitz)

Found while fixing H1 of PR #28: `crates/fulgur-vrt/fixtures/layout/multicol-table-width.html` (two panels in `column-count: 2`,
cells `width: 40% / 22% / 20% / 18%`) lays each table out wider than its column, so the two tables touch (second table at
x = 313 pt, the column starts at 283 pt); Chrome keeps each table in its column. The cause is that a cell's own percentage `width`
acts as its intrinsic size when the table is measured; ignoring the cell's `width` while measuring (what the H1 fix does for
length widths) gives the right result for this fixture (x = 283 pt), but the fork leaves percentages alone to keep the
`fulgur-vrt` golden byte-identical. When fixed, update that golden and say so.

### F74 — author `font-family: monospace` is 16 px where Chrome's is 13 px  (severity: low, size: small — ours)

Found while fixing F70. Stylo rescales a keyword-derived `font-size` (`medium` → 13 px) when the computed family list is
exactly the generic `monospace` (Blitz `font_metrics.rs:154`; Chrome does the same, and `monospace, monospace` is the known
way to switch it off). Since F70 the UA `pre` / `code` / `kbd` / `samp` / `tt` get it (10 `a` in `pre`: 58.5 pt, Chrome
13 px); an author `font-family: monospace` does not, because F3b rewrites the keyword to the family name `JetBrains Mono`
before the engine sees it (10 `a`: 72 pt at 16 px). Chrome: 13 px. Now that the engine resolves the six generics itself, the
F3b rewrite of those six (`monospace`, `serif`, `sans-serif`, `cursive`, `fantasy`, `system-ui`) is no longer needed and
dropping it fixes this; `ui-*`, `math`, `emoji`, `fangsong` must stay rewritten. Moves pixels only for documents that say
`font-family: monospace` with a keyword or default font size (Tailwind's `font-mono` lists `"JetBrains Mono"` first and is
not affected).

### F75 — text in a family that matches no face is dropped, where browsers fall back to a default face  (severity: medium, size: medium — fulgur / Parley)

Out of scope of F70 on purpose. `font-family: 'Nope'` (a name, not a generic) still gives no glyph run: F3's static check and,
since F70, the runtime check say so (warning; error under `--strict-fonts`), but the text is not in the PDF. Chrome falls back
to its default face (and then to any face with the glyph). Fontique's fallback map is empty without host fonts;
`Collection::append_fallbacks` with the first bundled family would render `'Nope'` in Inter instead of failing, which changes
F3's contract (an error today, a silent substitution after) — decide with an ADR, probably opt-in (`RendererBuilder::fallback_family`).

### F76 — the text of `<textarea>` and `<input>` is never painted  (severity: low, size: medium — Blitz / fulgur)

Found by the F70 probe (A6): with Inter registered, `<textarea>…</textarea>`, `<input value="…">` and the same with
`style="font-family:Inter"` leave no text in the PDF (`pdftotext` empty), while `<button>` and `<select>` text is drawn.
Blitz keeps the editor text of form controls outside the inline layout, and fulgur never paints it. Not a font problem, so
neither the generic map nor the dropped-text check sees it. Rare in invoices (static documents); a filled-in form would
lose its values.

### F67 — `<img src="data:...">` renders nothing  (severity: low, size: small — ours / fulgur)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #25: a data URI is not a bundled image and nothing is ever fetched, so the image is
absent; since F32a it is at least warned about (`not a bundled image`, an error under `--strict-images`). A decoded data URI
could be registered under its own text at `Renderer::render` (base64 / percent-decoding, size limit) and then goes through
`validate` like any other image. Low: a template engine can register the bytes instead.

### F33 — outside list markers (`list-style: disc` outside) are not painted  (severity: medium — Tailwind `list-disc` bullets missing, size: small — fulgur) — **done** (PR #20, `docs/plans/2026-10-16-f33-list-markers.md`)

Found while building fixture 06 (pre-existing, byte-identical at the base commit): `<ul><li>` with the default outside
disc paints no bullet; `list-style-position: inside` works, `<ol>` numbers work.

### F46 — an inline `<img>` inside a split paragraph is dropped and two lines merge  (severity: medium, size: small — fulgur)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #21 (pre-existing since F30: the image sits in a paragraph that `fragment_nested_inline_root`
splits): the `<img>` is not painted and the line it sat on is joined with the next one (`fc_img.html`,
`ctl_img_div.html` in `/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f30b/p/`). Descendants are recorded per strip (`record_split_inline_root_descendants`);
the inline image is probably not a descendant of the paragraph with a layout of its own there. Not reduced yet.

Extended by `vrt-validator` on PR #25 (F32a), `/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f32a/p/d3.html`: three paragraphs in a `div`, 150 inline-block
badges, split over 3 pages. After F32a we paint 148 of 150 (main: 48; Chrome 150): `B211` (mid-paragraph) and `B250` (the
last box of the last paragraph) are missing, and the page-1 / page-3 split differs from Chrome's (50 / 38 badges on
pages 1 / 3, Chrome 59 / 31) with garbled lines on page 3. Same family (inline boxes of a paragraph split by
`fragment_nested_inline_root`, placed by `record_split_inline_root_descendants`), so reduce them together; F32a reuses that
helper for body-level paragraphs, so a fix there serves both.

### F59 — a `colspan` wider than the table (the `colspan="99"` full-width row) breaks the column widths  (severity: medium — a common template idiom, size: medium — Blitz) — **done** (PR #26, `docs/plans/2026-10-17-f59-colspan-wider-than-table.md` §7)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #24 (pre-existing, identical on main): in a 3-column table with a full-width row
`<td colspan="99">`, the real columns collapse to the left edge (B1 at x = 72 pt, Chrome 196 pt). Already off by
2–29 pt with `colspan` 4 or 5. HTML's table model (§4.9.12) creates the spanned columns, but Chrome's auto layout gives
columns that only a span covers no width; Blitz turns each spanned column into an `auto` grid track that takes a share
of the width. Probe `/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f55/t/full99.html` (+ `full99.chrome.pdf`, PNGs `f99n-1.png` / `f99c-1.png`).
Also from that run: since F55 a row of 17 or more `colspan=1000` cells hits `MAX_COLUMNS` and the later cells share the
last column (`many20x5.html`; absurd input, documented in the F55 commit).

### F68 — a table with more than 32 767 rows loses the rest silently  (severity: medium — data loss, rare, size: medium — Blitz)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #26 (pre-existing): Blitz places table rows on Taffy grid lines, which are `i16`, so a
33 000-row table (`/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f59/a/rows33k.html`) prints 32 767 rows: 233 rows are lost without a warning
(222 pages, 26 s). At least warn; a real fix lays rows out in chunks or past the grid.

### F69 — a JPEG with a standalone marker in the header (TEM, RSTm, a second SOI) is not drawn  (severity: low, size: small — fulgur / zune-jpeg)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #27 (pre-existing): such files fail zune-jpeg's header read ("Not enough bytes") on main
and after F60, so a warning is logged and nothing is drawn; Chrome draws them. Probes `/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f60/atk/`
(TEM `FF 01`, RST0, double SOI). Also from the `pdf-reviewer` run: F23 confirmed — a raster `background-image` is drawn
4/3 too large (`64 0 0 48 cm` for a 64×48 px JPEG).

### F55 — `colspan=0` or a huge `colspan` panics the render  (severity: high — crash on valid HTML, size: small — Blitz) — **done** (PR #24, Blitz fork `b5f9df3`)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #23 (pre-existing, same exit code on main): `<table><tr><td colspan=0>A</td></tr></table>`
and `colspan=65535` panic inside Taffy (`track_sizing.rs:746`, `coordinates.rs:94`), so the whole document fails
(rc 1). HTML §4.9.11 / §13.2.6.4 ("rules for parsing non-negative integers" for `colspan`): a value of 0 is treated as
1 and values above 1000 are clamped to 1000; `rowspan` 0 means "to the end of the row group" and is clamped to 65534.
Probe `/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f50/x/cs0.html`.

### F56 — text clipped away with `clip: rect(0,0,0,0)` (Tailwind `sr-only`) is still in the PDF  (severity: low, size: small — fulgur)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #23: `sr-only` text is invisible but extractable (`pdftotext` shows it), in `<span>`s
and, since F50, in `td.sr-only` cells. Chrome omits it from the PDF. `clip` on absolutely positioned boxes is not
applied. Probe `/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f50/x/sronly.html`.

### F57 — `display: list-item` numbering is off by one  (severity: low, size: small — Blitz)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #23 (pre-existing): a second `display: list-item` div is numbered `3.` instead of `2.`
(Chrome `2.`). Probably the same counter start as F41.

### F58 — the print viewport for `@media (min-width)` is ≈ 680 px, Chrome's ≈ 740 px  (severity: low, size: small — fulgur)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #23: `@media (min-width: 700px)` and `720px` evaluate differently from Chrome on A4;
at ≤ 640 and ≥ 760 both agree, so Tailwind `md:` (768) is off in both. Check which width Chrome uses for print media
queries (page box minus margins vs. the default 15 mm margins) before changing ours.

### F49 — the legacy `<td height>` attribute is ignored  (severity: low — Tailwind uses classes, size: small — Blitz)

Found while fixing F48 (pre-existing): Blitz does not map `<td height="60">` to a CSS `height` (HTML §15.3.8 maps it
like `width`); Chrome gives the row 68 px, ours 28 (probe `out/scratch/f48/p7.html`). Same family as F27 (`valign`).

### F50 — `td { display: block | flex }` drops the cell's content and collapses the row  (severity: high — data loss, size: medium — Blitz) — **done**, see Status

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #22 (pre-existing): a table cell whose `display` is changed to `block` or `flex` (a Tailwind
`block` / `flex` on a `td` or `th`) loses its content entirely and the row collapses (`tb_blk_noh.html`,
`tb_flex_noh.html`, `cell_blockdisp.html`, `cell_flex.html` in `/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f48/s/`): the cell after it is at
47 pt where Chrome has 128. Not reduced yet; probably the cell is no longer a table item but the table wrapper still
sizes the row from the grid items only.

### F50b — what F50 leaves: missing parents, `table { display: block }`, `tr.block` cells side by side  (severity: medium, size: medium — Blitz)

Pushed out of F50 (`2026-10-16-f50-cell-display-override.md` §7, probes under `out/scratch/f50/`):

- **Missing parents** (CSS 2.1 §17.2.1 rule 3): a `td` whose `display` is still `table-cell` but whose parent is a
  `tr { display: block | flex }` (P5: Chrome puts `U1 U2` side by side in a nested anonymous table, we stack them; with
  `tr.flex` the flex layout happens to match), a cell or row outside any table, a `tbody.block` holding several cells.
  The place is `construct.rs` `_` arm (a table-part `display` in a non-table parent).
- **`table { display: block }`** (P8, P8w): its `tbody`/`tr` reach the `_` arm, are laid out as Taffy's default flex
  (S1 S2 S3 on one line), and the whitespace text nodes become layout children that `layout/mod.rs:142-150` reports with
  `println!` ("ERROR: Tried to lay out text node individually", 2028 bytes on stdout for a 12-line table).
- The same arm also calls `dbg!(data)` (`layout/mod.rs:149`), which dumps the `TextNodeData` to **stderr**; it is upstream
  Blitz 0.2.4 code (`blitz-dom-0.2.4/src/layout/mod.rs:128`, the fork only moved it in `bb18d4c`), not ours. Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #25.
- Related stdout noise: `table_part_of` keeps the old `println!` for a table child without styles; fixture 02 prints
  "ERROR: Unexpected token" from its CSS (unrelated, pre-existing).
- Not done: `white-space: pre` text in a run (the anonymous cell's whitespace trimming ignores it), a second `<caption>`,
  `inline-table`, `rowspan` (F15).
- Seen in the probes, not caused by F50: `display: inline` cells get no horizontal padding (`IAIB` merge, P4); an
  `inline-block` sits 6.6 pt lower than Chrome's in any cell (p4c); a nested `display: table` keeps the `td` padding where
  Chrome ignores it (R5b); auto column widths with an anonymous cell differ from Chrome's (p2 `C4` at 382 pt, Chrome 354).

### F51 — `overflow: hidden | auto | scroll` on a `td` keeps the row from growing to its content  (severity: medium — content overlaps or is lost, size: medium — Blitz)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #22 (pre-existing, without any `height`): the row is not as tall as the cell's content
(`ovx_hidden_none.html`: the next row at 68 pt, Chrome 128), so what follows overlaps it; a 120-line cell
(`mh_td_hidden_120.html`) loses 67 words, has 4 overlaps and prints 1 page where Chrome prints 3. Tailwind's
`max-h-8 overflow-hidden` on a cell (`tw_maxh.html`) is the likely invoice shape. `overflow: clip` is fine, so it is
Taffy's automatic minimum size of a scroll container (zero) applied to the cell as a grid item.

### F52 — an image cell's row ignores the image's height  (severity: low, size: small — Blitz)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #22 (pre-existing): `img_known_ctl.html`: the next row at 68 pt, Chrome 113.

### F53 — extra height of a `table { height }` is distributed over the rows differently from Chrome  (severity: low, size: small — Blitz)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #22 (pre-existing; `u_zero_pct_tblh.html` and family). F48 only makes the table at least
as tall as its `height`; how the extra is shared between rows (CSS 2.1 §17.5.3 leaves it to the UA) is not Chrome's in
every shape. Also: a `<td>` with `height` and `rowspan` does not distribute the height over the spanned rows (`rs1.html`;
belongs to F15's rowspan work).
From the second validation of PR #22 (low): a table with percentage padding and a `height` larger than its content is
too tall by the padding — `grid_container_min_height` counts a `%` padding as 0 while Taffy adds the resolved one
(`/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f48/s/r2/pctpad_bor.html`: AFTER 244.6 pt, Chrome 193.8; main matched only by accident, as the same
table without `height` is already ≈ 51 pt too tall — percentage padding on a table is itself wrong); a
`border-collapse` + `content-box` table with a 6 px border and a `height` is 6 px too tall (112 vs 106 px, same as
main; Chrome seems to count half the collapsed border).

### F54 — `td { height: 100000px }` gives 2 pages where Chrome gives 100  (severity: low, size: small — fulgur / Blitz)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #22 (pre-existing; `u_huge.html`): an absurdly tall cell is not paginated across its height.

### F47 — a single-side `border-bottom` / `-left` / `-right` on a table cell is painted black  (severity: medium — Tailwind `border-b` works only through the colour utilities, size: unknown — Blitz / fulgur)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #21 (pre-existing): `td { border-bottom: 3px solid blue }` paints black
(`bb_plain.html`). Checked while fixing F30b with `border-collapse: separate`, 96 dpi: on a cell `border-bottom`,
`border-left` and `border-right` alone paint black, `border-top` alone and `border: 3px solid blue` (also as
`border-color` + `border-style`) paint blue, and the same single-side borders on a `div` are blue. So it is the cell
box with a single non-top side, not every cell border.

### F48 — a fixed `height` smaller than the content on a cell whose content is taller than a page cuts the text  (severity: high — data loss, size: medium — fulgur / Blitz) — **done** (PR #22, `docs/plans/2026-10-16-f48-fixed-height-cell.md`)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #21 (pre-existing, identical in main): `td { height: 40px }` (a height smaller than the
content, which CSS 2.1 §17.5.3 treats as a minimum) on a cell with a 120-line `<p>`: 53 lines on 1 page and what
follows the table is drawn at y = 80 over the content (Chrome: 120 lines on 3 pages, AFTER at y = 347); with both cells
`height: 40px` 94 words are lost (`z_tdh_one`, `y_tdh_small` in `/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f30b/p/`). `tr { height }` and
`td { height >= 1500px }` are fine, so the cell is probably laid out with the small height as its box and fragmented
as a leaf (`child_h` is the 40 px, not the content). Not reduced yet.

**Done** (plan `2026-10-16-f48-fixed-height-cell.md` §7): the cell's own style kept the `height` (Blitz clamped the cell box
to it); fixed in the Blitz fork, no fulgur change.

### F41 — `<ul style="list-style-type: decimal">` numbers from 2  (severity: low, size: small — Blitz)

Found while fixing F33: `layout/construct.rs:203` starts the list counter at `1` for every container but `ol`
(`(1, false)`), so a `ul`/`menu` with a numbering `list-style-type` prints `2.`, `3.`, … (Chrome: `1.`, `2.`).
Bullets are unaffected. Not a template idiom (`ol` is right), hence low.

### F42 — inline text before a block child is painted after the child's content and is untagged  (severity: medium — reading order and PDF/UA, size: medium — fulgur)

Found by `pdf-reviewer` on PR #20 (pre-existing, independent of markers): `<div>Text před blokem<div>Vnitřní
blok</div></div>` paints the anonymous inline text after the descendants in the content stream, and that text is
neither tagged nor an artifact (PDF/UA-1 §7.1). Positions are right; copy order and the structure tree are not. In
nested lists the parent items' text is missing from the tagged tree. Probes `/tmp/claude-0/pdfrev-f33/probes/mixed.html`,
`nested.html`, `nested-none.html`. F20 covers only table cells and margin boxes.

### F43 — list tagging: empty `/Lbl` for `list-style: none`, inside markers inside `/LBody`  (severity: low, size: small — fulgur)

Found by `pdf-reviewer` on PR #20 (pre-existing): an item without a marker still gets an empty `/Lbl`; an `inside`
marker is tagged as part of `/LBody` ("• Uvnitř"). Not a spec violation; omit `Lbl` when there is no marker and put
inside markers in `Lbl`.

### F44 — a glyph missing from the bundled face is painted as `.notdef` without warning  (severity: medium — PDF/A, size: small — fulgur)

Found by `pdf-reviewer` on PR #20 (pre-existing): inside markers and running text (`A◦B▪C` with Noto Sans) paint GID 0
— a visible box, one of them without `ActualText` — and `--strict-fonts` does not catch it (PDF/A-2 §6.2.11.8,
PDF/UA-1 §7.21.8). F33 guards outside markers only. Detect at shaping time: warn by default, refuse in strict mode.
Probe `/tmp/claude-0/pdfrev-f33/probes/notdef.html`.

### F45 — a 165-page invoice takes 2.2 s; target is 200 pages under 1 s  (severity: medium — product goal 2, size: unknown)

Measured 2026-10-16 with the release CLI: fixture 02's rows repeated to ≈4 000 rows → 165 pages in 2.1–2.4 s (best of
3, process start and font loading included). Single-page fixtures are 23–30 ms (01–07: 23, 59, 27, 26, 30, 28, 39 ms).
Not profiled yet: split the time between Stylo, table layout, the fragmenter and the writer before choosing a fix.

### F34 — `flex justify-end` around a fixed-width table misplaces it and drops a `border-t-2` total row  (severity: medium, size: unknown)

Found while drafting fixture 06 (pre-existing): a `w-72` totals table inside `flex justify-end` sat ≈37 px right of the
content edge and its `border-t-2` row rule was missing. Not reduced yet.

### F35 — cells next to a split cell: `vertical-align: middle/bottom` text lost, row/cell backgrounds wrong  (severity: medium, size: medium — Blitz F13 offset × fulgur fragmenter)

F83 `vrt-validator`: `<tr><td>{700 words}</td><td>word99991</td></tr>` (default `vertical-align: middle`, with or without `break-inside: avoid` on the `td` / `tr`) loses the short cell's text on main and the branch
(`/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f83/att/cases3/td.html`, `tr_avoid.html`).

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #18 (pre-existing, same in the pre-F30 build): in a row whose `<td><p>` is taller than a
page, a sibling cell with `vertical-align: middle` or `bottom` loses its text (its offset lands past the page); a
`<tr>` background on sibling cells runs to the page edge on page 1 and is missing on continuation pages (Chrome tints
the whole row on every page). Probes `out/scratch/val/h1-*.html`, `d2-td-p-middle.html`.
Same family, seen by `vrt-validator` on PR #21 with a `tr { background }` and a tall cell (`trbg.html`): the fill is
painted per cell, so the shorter neighbour's column has a page-tall fill on page 1 (it runs past the page bottom) and
none on pages 2–3 (a gap beside the tall cell). Not introduced by F30b: the `<td><p>` form of the same table is
byte-identical on main and with F30b; F30b only makes the direct-text form print all its lines (main lost them).
Also pre-existing (PR #21, `ctl_td_two_p.html`, `next_row_two_p.html` in `/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f30b/p/`): a row of two
`<td><p>` cells that both spill past page 1 (A 120 lines, B 80) pushes B's last 2 lines to page 3 (50 + 28 + 2 instead of
Chrome's 50 + 30; the widow pull-back leaves the last two lines of the shorter cell on the last page). No line is lost.
Where the content after the table starts was fixed in F30b (y ≈ 347 px like Chrome, was 677): a row ends at the content of
its cells, not at the stretch below a shorter cell. The direct-text form of the same row matches Chrome.
Still open from the third validation round: `vertical-align: bottom | middle` on a shorter cell next to a split cell
distorts its text (`y_vbottom_short`: B starts at the top of page 1 with 10 lines and continues 50 + 20 on pages 2–3,
Chrome 50 + 50 + 30; same as main); a middle cell with a large `padding-bottom` that outlasts the tallest one ends the
row at the tallest cell's content, not at its padding (`y_pb300_three`: AFTER at y = 347, Chrome 414; was 527 before
the row-end fixes, 44 / overlapping in main).
From the fourth round (PR #21, low, no loss or overlap): a negative `margin-bottom` on a split cell's last child is
ignored (`w_neg`: AFTER 344, Chrome 324); an empty last child's margins are not counted (`w_empty`: 347, Chrome 384);
a `<ul>` whose `<li>` have `margin-bottom:16px` in a split cell leaves ≈ 200 pt of blank space before AFTER (`w_ul`:
680, Chrome 479; main overlapped). Probes `/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f30b/p/w_*.html`.

### F36 — `<tfoot>` is printed right under the header, before the body rows  (severity: medium — visible on any table with a footer, size: small — Blitz) — **done** (`2026-10-19-f36-tfoot-order.md` §7)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #18 (pre-existing): Blitz places table cells in DOM order, so a `<tfoot>` written before
`<tbody>` (the HTML4 pattern) renders between header and body; Chrome renders it at the bottom (and repeats it per
page, F12b). Probes `out/scratch/val/d6*.html`, `e1-foot-short.html`.

### F37 — page-0 body background / border extends the body offset below the content box  (severity: medium, size: small — fulgur render) — **more visible since F184** (2026-10-27): with `html { background: #eee } body { background: #fff; margin: 40px }` (grey page, white card, a likely invoice pattern) body's own box now stands out stretched to the page bottom where Chrome shows a content-high card (F184 vrt-validator); raise its priority. — **done** (`2026-10-27-f37-body-own-background.md`)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #19 (pre-existing): `body{margin:40px;background:#ffd}` + 130 `<p>` → the background on
page 1 ends at 1105 px instead of the content bottom (1065.8 px, Chrome 1065); with a body border, page 1 shows a
closed frame 40 px too low. The page-0 body frame is sliced to the full page height, then shifted by `body_offset`
(the F29 fix covered content, not the body frame). Probes `/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f29/bgonly.html`, `bodybg.html`.

### F38 — first content taller than page 0's room / offset ≥ page height  (severity: medium — content off the page, size: medium — fulgur)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #19 (pre-existing, F29 non-goals): a first `<img>` or `break-inside: avoid` block
taller than page 0's room overflows into the bottom margin (Chrome moves it to page 2); with an offset ≥ the page
height (`body{margin:1200px 0 0}`) 50 of 60 paragraphs are painted off the page (a warning is logged). Probes
`img-first.html`, `mono-first-avoid.html`, `huge-*.html`.

### F39 — a box whose border/padding fit but whose first line doesn't is split with a squashed frame  (severity: low–medium, size: small — fulgur)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #19 (pre-existing): a bordered, padded box with one paragraph right above a page end has
its top border drawn on page 1 and its text on page 2; Chrome moves the box whole. Also: when a box's closing padding
doesn't fit, fulgur moves the whole box, where Chrome splits between its children. Probes `squash.py`, `boxsplit.py`.

### F40 — fixed-position elements on pages after the first sit `body_offset` too high  (severity: low, size: small — fulgur render) — **done** (F187: `2026-10-27-f187-abspos-body-margin.md`)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #19 (pre-existing): `position: fixed; top: 0` is at y = −1.4 pt (off the page) on pages
2+ when the body has a top offset; `left: 0` anchors to the page instead of the content box. Probe `fixed.html`.

### F26 — `vertical-align: baseline` in table cells is treated as `top`  (severity: low, size: medium — Blitz)

Split from F13. CSS 2.1 §17.5.3: baseline cells align their first-line baselines and the row may grow. Differs from
`top` only when a row's baseline cells have different first-baseline offsets (fonts, padding, borders).

### F27 — the `valign` attribute on `td`/`th`/`tr` is ignored  (severity: low — Tailwind uses classes, size: small — Blitz)

Split from F13. HTML §15.3.8 maps `valign` to `vertical-align`; Blitz's attribute mapping (`stylo.rs`) only maps
`align`.

### F12b — `<tfoot>` is not repeated at the bottom of each page  (severity: low — no fixture, size: medium — fulgur)

Split from F12. Blitz places footer cells in DOM order (a `tfoot` before `tbody` lands on top), and repeating it
needs space reserved at the bottom of every page but the last.

Amended by F36 (2026-10-19): Blitz now places the first footer group last, wherever it is written, so the footer cells are a suffix of the table's
`layout_children` (the mirror of F12's header prefix). A `tfoot` is still printed once, on the last page only; Chrome repeats it on every page (probes
`t2long` / `t7`: the same footer after the last row of each page). The ¼-page policy and the reserved band are still to do.

### F16 — `none` across colour-space conversions in `color-mix()`, relative colours and gradients  (severity: low — Tailwind never emits `none`, size: medium — Stylo / fulgur)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #12 (F11). Stylo 0.8.0 `AbsoluteColor::to_color_space` carries a missing component as
NaN instead of applying css-color-4's analogous-component rules, so `color-mix(in oklab, oklch(70% 0.2 none), …)`,
`rgb(from oklch(70% 0.2 none) r g b)` and `color-mix(in srgb, white, oklch(none none none))` differ from Chrome; legacy
`rgb()` `none` and `none` alpha lose "missing" semantics in mixes. fulgur gradients neither carry a `none` stop
component from the neighbouring stop (css-color-4 §12) nor honour `in <space>`. Probes `/tmp/claude-0/vrt-f11/`
(`probe.py c3.txt bg`, `grad.py c4.txt g1`).

### F17 — non-sRGB colours in SVG presentation attributes and SVG CSS paint black  (severity: medium, size: small — Blitz)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #12. `<rect fill="oklch(…)">`, `style="fill:oklch(…)"`, `stop-color="oklch(…)"` and
`<style>rect{fill:currentColor}</style>` with an `oklch()` `color` paint black: usvg parses these itself and does not
know `oklch()`/`lab()`. The F1 fix only covers `currentColor` serialised for the `<svg>` element. Tailwind
`fill-*`/`stroke-*` utilities on SVG children emit `oklch()`. Expected: the sRGB equivalent, as in Chrome.

### F18 — paint gaps seen while probing colours  (severity: medium for 05, size: unknown — fulgur)

Found by `vrt-validator` on PR #12, unrelated to colour conversion: `box-shadow` blur is not rendered (hard-edged
shadow; Tailwind `shadow-*` all blur, hits 05); `outline` paints nothing; `opacity` on an inline `<svg>` is applied
twice (`.5` → 0.25); an SVG `data:` URI background with `currentColor` renders blank. Split when one is picked up.

## Status

- **F3 done** (PR #1): built-in last-resort mono face, default `html { font-family }` prelude from the first bundled
  font, static scan that warns (strict opt-in). Still open as **F3b**: generic families alone (`monospace`,
  `sans-serif`) are not mapped — needs a fulgur change (fork + patch). Scanner blind spots are listed in the plan.
- **F2 done** (polyfill, see `2026-10-15-f2-property-defaults.md`): `@property` initial values from bundled CSS are
  applied by a generated lowest-priority layer. Borders, dividers, card shadows and rings render; `--tw-leading` &c.
  no longer leak to children. Root cause found: Stylo 0.8.0 has `@property` compiled in for servo but its parser is
  gated by `layout.css.properties-and-values.enabled`, which `stylo_static_prefs` 0.8.0 does not list (→ false).
  Open as **F2b**: enable it upstream (Blitz bump of Stylo, or fork `stylo_static_prefs` + `[patch.crates-io]`), then
  keep the polyfill only as a fallback. Gradients are still missing — that is F8, not F2. Borders on `<tr>`
  (01's row rules) are still absent: they paint only in the collapsing border model, Blitz #386.
- **F1 done** via fork branches (`2026-10-15-f1-srgb-conversion.md` §7): 01 5.3→4.4, 03 10.1→8.9, 04 9.9→5.8,
  05 32.3→21.5 %.
- **F10 done** (see PR): CSS that ends inside an open block is closed by `htmltopdf` (CSS Syntax 3 §5.4 does the same
  at end of input), so fulgur's `::marker` pre-pass no longer panics; any remaining engine panic is caught at the API
  boundary and returned as `Error::EnginePanic`. Upstream bug (bounds check in `rewrite_marker_content_url`) not reported; a fork fix is possible.
- **F4 done** in the Blitz fork (`2026-10-15-f4-text-transform.md`): `text-transform` case transforms apply.
- **F28 done** in the fulgur fork (`2026-10-16-f28-consecutive-tables.md`): a box whose first row does not fit moves to
  the next page before it is entered; 60 one-row tables paginate like Chrome, no row lost.
- **F9 done** in the fulgur + Blitz forks (`2026-10-16-f9-img-natural-size.md`): `<img>` without width/height
  attributes renders at its natural size (03 logo back, 6.2 → 5.7 %); block-level images are not stretched.
- **F30 done** (paragraph sub-case) in the fulgur fork (`2026-10-16-f30-nested-paragraph-split.md`): a paragraph
  taller than a page inside a block splits between line boxes instead of losing its tail; new fixture 06.
- **F29 done** in the fulgur fork (`2026-10-16-f29-body-offset.md`): page 0 is paginated against its real room (body
  margin / collapsed first-child margin / `html` padding); nothing hangs below the content box; new fixture 07.
- **F33 done** in the Blitz fork (`2026-10-16-f33-list-markers.md`): outside disc / circle / square markers are painted
  (the marker is shaped with the item's own font when Blitz's `Bullet` face is not registered); 06 uses the default
  outside disc again, 06 p2 12.5 → 11.8 %; a symbol the item's font lacks is not painted (fulgur fork guard, no
  `.notdef` box). Found **F41** (`ul` + `decimal` numbers from 2) and, in review, **F42**–**F44**; **F45** (speed).
- **F30b done** in the fulgur fork (`2026-10-16-f30b-split-in-flex-grid-cells.md`): a paragraph taller than a page
  that is a flex item, a grid item or a direct-text table cell splits between line boxes (`flexcol_p`, `grid1_p`,
  `td_direct1` 53 lines → all, 7 / 7 / 6 pages like Chrome); a single-column flex / grid box closes below the split
  item; a `<tr>` background covers a split cell on every page; new fixture 08 (2 / 2 pages, 13.2 / 12.1 %). Rows
  with several items: **F30h**.
- **F55 done** in the Blitz fork (`b5f9df3`): `colspan` parsed by HTML's rules for non-negative integers and clamped
  to 1..=1000 (Chrome agrees on 12 edge values); a row's cells stay within 16384 columns (Taffy's grid lines are
  `i16`; later cells share the last column). `colspan=0`, `65535` and 40 × `colspan=1000` rendered rc 1 before.
  `crates/htmltopdf/tests/table_spans.rs` (5 tests, 4 red before); fixtures 01–10 byte-identical. `rowspan` is still
  ignored (F15), so its values cannot crash.
- **F50 done** in the Blitz and fulgur forks (`2026-10-16-f50-cell-display-override.md` §7): a table part whose `display`
  is not a table display is wrapped in an anonymous cell / row (CSS 2.1 §17.2.1) instead of being dropped; `td.block`,
  `td.flex`, `tr.block`, `tbody.block` keep their content and the row its height; a row background shows behind the
  anonymous cell. New fixture 10 (2 / 2 pages like Chrome, 5.9 / 1.5 %; before the fix 1 page, items missing). What is
  left is **F50b** (missing parents, `table { display: block }`, stdout `println!`).
- **F32a done** in the Blitz and fulgur forks and ours (`2026-10-17-f32a-oversized-and-broken-images.md` §7): the image
  that vanished was not lost in Parley but never recorded by the fragmenter — every paragraph of two or more lines
  (Parley adds an empty second line after an overflowing box, but also any ordinary wrap) got no geometry for its inline
  boxes, so an `<img>` or `inline-block` in it was laid out and never painted; fixed in `fragment_inline_root`, and the
  empty line is taken back in Blitz (`break_lines`; no Parley fork). A 700 px image in a 680 px content box now paints
  over the margin and the next block starts below it (was: nothing, and a gap of twice the height). A PNG that does not
  decode no longer fails the whole render (`image::validate` before Krilla, 128 Mpx limit); `strict_images` and warnings
  for unregistered / empty / undecodable `<img src>`. Fixtures 01-10 byte-identical, compare numbers unchanged. Chrome
  shrinks an overflowing page to fit instead of overflowing; we follow CSS (§7). F46 (nested split paragraph) is a
  different path and still open. Rest of F32: **F32b**-**F32m**. Found in review of its PR: **F60**-**F67**, an extension of **F20** and of **F46**.
- **F59 done** in the Blitz fork (`ee5477b`, `2026-10-17-f59-colspan-wider-than-table.md` §7): in automatic layout the
  grid tracks are the columns some cell starts in and a cell spans the tracks inside its range, so a `colspan="99"` row no
  longer adds 96 `auto` tracks that squeezed the real columns to the left edge (B1 at 72 pt, Chrome 196 pt; now 196 pt).
  Chrome gives a column no cell starts in no width (HTML calls it a table model error); `table-layout: fixed` keeps every
  column only when the table has a width (Chrome lays out `fixed; width: auto` automatically). `colspan` 4 / 5, 20 ×
  `colspan=1000` (F55 shared the last four), an inner span-only column and nested tables match their exact-span twins.
  New fixture 11 (1 / 1 page like Chrome; 8.1 % → 6.9 % against Chrome, columns within 0.25 pt of Chrome's); fixtures 01-10
  byte-identical; `table_spans.rs` 17 tests (8 red before), 10 Blitz unit tests. Left in **F15**: dividing a spanning
  first-row cell's width over its columns, `<col>` widths, fixed layout dividing the width equally whatever the content,
  `border-spacing`.
- **F60 done** in the fulgur fork (`f5ef832`, `2026-10-17-f60-jpeg-fill-bytes.md` §7): fulgur's own header scan
  (`ImageRender::decode_dimensions`), not Krilla, took the first `0xFF` fill byte for the marker; it now skips them, so a
  JPEG with fill bytes has its natural size again (`<img>` 100 px wide: 100 x 75 as in Chrome, was a 100 x 0 box and
  nothing painted; `background-image`: natural size, was 1 x 1). A header the scan cannot read the way the decoder does is
  reported as `damaged` instead of silently dropped. Fixtures 01-11 byte-identical, compare numbers unchanged;
  `image_robustness.rs` 3 new tests (all red before), 1 guard each in `image_warnings.rs` / `image_strict.rs`.
- **F66 done** in the Blitz fork (`aebe118`, `2026-10-17-f66-white-space-nowrap.md` §7; tests in the fulgur fork `f7e3104`):
  `white-space: nowrap` / `pre` (and `text-wrap-mode: nowrap`) no longer soft-wrap: the IBAN cell of an invoice table stays
  on one line inside its table (x 232-454 px, Chrome 233-455; was two rows), a 418 px `nowrap` line overflows start-aligned
  like Chrome's, `<pre>` keeps its long lines, `truncate` clips at the box. `pre-line` collapses the indentation (rows start
  at x 58, Chrome 58; were at 73) and `td[nowrap]` works. Fixtures 01-11 byte-identical, compare numbers unchanged,
  `white_space.rs` 9 tests (7 red before), 4 fork tests. Open: **F66b**-**F66f** (F66f, a trailing newline adds an empty
  line, is the one that moves real documents).
  **PR #28 review (`vrt-validator`):** H1 fixed in the Blitz fork (`204c4c2`): an explicit cell `width` below the cell's
  min-content (`nowrap` text, a long word) no longer spills into the next cell, the column widens like Chrome's (fixtures
  01-02, 04-11 byte-identical; 03's "Množství" column is 88 px like Chrome's instead of 80, 5.68 -> 5.68 %); H2 became F66g
  with a per-page warning (fulgur `140000c`). Filed from the same run: **F70** (HIGH: bare `<pre>` / `<code>` / `<kbd>` /
  `<samp>` / `<tt>` drop their text with only Inter bundled), F71 (tab in `pre`), F72 (`::before` trailing space), F73
  (percentage cell widths in multicol), notes under F66 (RTL, `<wbr>`) and F66b (`<nobr>`, `normal` span).
  **Second round (R1):** a *percentage* column width (`10%`, Tailwind `w-1/6` = `calc(1/6 * 100%)`) no longer cuts `nowrap` text either (Blitz `52b22ef`):
  `nowrap` "12 345 678,00 Kč" in a `10%` cell of a 400 px table: the next column starts at 172 px, Chrome 173 (was 97, spilling); an unbreakable
  word in `5%` / `20%` cells now matches Chrome exactly (260 px, was 157). Fixtures 01-11 byte-identical to the previous head. Open (F15):
  `colspan` cells with a width, percentages summing past 100 %, R2 (`k3`). Side finding in F6: `@page { margin: 0 }` is overridden by the 15 mm default.
- **F70 done** in the fulgur fork (`c9230dc`) and ours (`2026-10-17-f70-ua-monospace.md` §7): a bare `<pre>`, `<code>`,
  `<kbd>`, `<samp>`, `<tt>`, `<xmp>`, `<listing>` is drawn in the built-in JetBrains Mono (or the face `generic_family(Monospace, …)`
  names) with only Inter bundled; was: all its text missing, rc 0, no warning even with `--strict-fonts`. The engine resolves the
  generics itself and reports text that no glyph run covers (`log::warn!` per distinct text; `Error::TextWithoutFont` under
  `strict_fonts`), which also finds what the static scan cannot see (a `var()` that is `'Nope'` where it is used). Fixtures 01-11
  byte-identical, compare numbers unchanged, zero dropped-text warnings on them. Filed: **F74** (author `monospace` is 16 px,
  Chrome 13 px), **F75** (named family with no face still drops text), **F76** (`<textarea>` / `<input>` text never painted). Review of

- **F154 + F159(f) done** in the Blitz fork (`d04479a`, after `b4cbc77`) (`2026-10-21-f154-span-edge-whitespace.md` §7): white space collapses across the boundaries of inline elements and is removed at the start and end of a line and around forced breaks (CSS Text 3 §4.1.1, §4.1.3), so `<b>Splatnost: </b>2. 11. 2026` and `content: "Pred "` keep their space and an NBSP at an element's edge is no longer deleted; fixture 22 3.5 % against Chrome (main's binary 4.0 %, every label glued to its value); fixtures 01-21, the bench corpus and the scaling variants byte-identical to main (94 of 94). New findings F166 (nested fixed tables lose words, found by the fuzz test), F167 (inside list marker width).
- **F150 + F153 done** in the Blitz fork (`0819dd4`, `b4cbc77`, after `d5d60b7`) and the fulgur fork (`2dc4c2b`, a test only) (`2026-10-20-f150-display-contents-construction.md` §7): the children, `::before` and `::after` of a `display: contents` element are placed as children of the container, so a block inside one is laid out (F153: its text was lost silently) and the whitespace-block cleanup cannot free a listed box (F150: "invalid key" panic); fixture 21 3.7 % against Chrome (main's binary 7.4 % with most of the page missing), 1 page like Chrome; fixtures 01-20, the bench corpus and the scaling variants byte-identical to main (92 of 92). New findings F154 (Parley trims the whitespace at the edges of every inline element, medium-high), F155-F160. The review of the branch found two defects of the change itself, fixed in the fulgur fork (`96ebb8a`, `5c9bae5`): a tagged render panicked in krilla when one link became several boxes (newly reachable through `<a style="display:contents">`; also on main with `<a><p>A</p><div>B</div></a>`), and multicol containers dropped the content of a wrapper, or the flow after a wrapped `column-span: all` (the multicol walks and predicates read DOM children). More findings from the review: F161-F164.
- **F161 + F165 done** in the fulgur fork (`5aa953f6`, after `182ffb7`) and ours (`2026-10-21-f161-multicol-lost-words.md`): a paragraph that starts in a column with less than one line of room is no longer cut after its first line and overwritten in the next column; a multicol container with `column-span: all` children that does not fit its page is cut by the F30d fragmenter over its segments (column rows and spanners); the content after a multicol container is moved through a `display: contents` sibling. New fixture 24 (`24-columns-span`, 2 pages like Chrome, 8.6 %; main's binary loses 86 of its 948 words), `multicol_lost_words.rs`, the multicol floor of the `display_contents.rs` fuzz test is back; fixtures 01-23 byte-identical to main. Review fixes in fork `1c1c164b` and `49e16712` (a box that does not fit a partial page moves to the next one; a spanner container with a box taller than a page or a spanner taller than a quarter page keeps the old path): 2200 fuzz documents against main, 0 print fewer words, 33 more. Filed **F173** (leftovers, and six pre-existing lossy fuzz seeds).
- **F163c done** in the Blitz fork (`f4132d5`, after `71de9c0`) (`2026-10-26-f163c-table-cell-pseudo-thead.md` §7): Blitz sorted the layout children of every Taffy Grid node by `order`, a table included; a `display: table-cell` `::before` (order `i32::MIN`) moved in front of the header cells and fulgur no longer repeated the `thead`, and an author `order` on a cell reordered the fragmenter's walk (8 of 266 words printed). A table root's layout children are no longer sorted. `table_pseudo.rs` T18a-d against Chrome 148 (3 pages, header on each); fixtures 01-24 and the bench corpus byte-identical to main.
- **F174 done** in the fulgur fork (`fa457165`, `d281ee1d`, `f8adc101`, `5f831cd2`, `c41518b5`, after `49e16712`) and ours (`2026-10-26-f174-table-in-columns.md` §7): a table in a multicol container is cut between its rows by the column flow (row bands as atoms of the F30d planner), its header repeated at the top of every column piece; collapsed borders are drawn per piece at the piece's x, row backgrounds once, each piece is a closed box; a container that fits its page is balanced across its columns too. The 130-row shape prints 269 of 269 words like Chrome (main 107); new fixture 25 (a payment schedule in two-column terms, 2 pages like Chrome, 8.3 %); `multicol_tables.rs` A-G against Chrome 148; fixtures 01-24 and the bench corpus byte-identical to main. Fork `c41518b5` review fix: every piece but the last is closed by the bottom border below its rows. vrt-validator: about 390 documents (90 hand-written, 300 fuzz), no regression against main, multicol documents without tables byte-identical; pdf-reviewer: no PDF defect. Filed F174b (shrink-to-fit), F174c (a table inside a wrapper or with a caption), F174d (a container with a table moved to the next page), F174e (follow-ups).
- **F177 done** in the Blitz fork (`b9a0fa8c`, after `f4132d5`) and the fulgur fork (`bb91c75e`, `7dc11600`, after `6069832d`) (`2026-10-26-f177-svg-image-checks.md` §7): every raster inside an SVG (inline `<svg>`, an SVG `<img>`, `background-image`, `list-style-image`, in `<pattern>` / `<mask>` / `feImage` too) is checked by a usvg href resolver with F32a's decoders and pixel limit before krilla sees it: one that does not decode or is too large is left out with a warning naming the SVG, `strict_images(true)` refuses the document (`ImageProblemKind::InSvg`), a damaged JPEG is embedded with a warning. A PNG header claiming 100000 x 100000 no longer aborts the process; a truncated raster no longer fails the whole document. Valid rasters byte-identical; fixtures 01-25 and the corpus byte-identical to main. Margin-box SVGs are checked and warned but not refused in strict mode (they are converted after the engine reports): F177c.
- **F177b done** in the fulgur fork (`70ab4525`, after `7dc11600`) (`2026-10-26-f177b-svg-href-offline.md` §7, ADR-0006): no SVG parse reads the filesystem any more. An inline `<svg>`'s `<image href>` is looked up by name among the bundled images (the `<img src>` rule), an SVG used as an image loads no external href, anything else is left out with a warning; `strict_images(true)` refuses it (`ImageProblemKind::UnregisteredInSvg`). `href="/dev/zero"` no longer hangs (also in the bundled-image check before the engine runs). `svg_image_offline.rs` 5 tests; fixtures 01-25 and the corpus byte-identical to main. Filed F177d.

- **F48 done** in the Blitz fork (`2026-10-16-f48-fixed-height-cell.md`): a table cell's or table's length `height` is a
  minimum (a table's counts border and padding like Chrome: the total under `border-box`, the content box under
  `content-box`) and `max-height` is ignored on both, applied to the cell's own style as well as to its grid-item style, so a
  `h-12` / `height: 40px` cell no longer clips or overlaps its content and a cell with a paragraph taller than a page is
  entered by the fragmenter (`z_tdh_one` 53 → 120 lines, `y_tdh_small` 94 lost words → none); new fixture 09 (3 / 3 pages
  like Chrome, 5.1 / 4.9 / 0.7 %; before the fix 2 / 3 pages, rows overlapping).
- **F13 done** in the Blitz fork (`2026-10-16-f13-cell-vertical-align.md`): `vertical-align: middle` (the default) and
  `bottom` align cell content; 02 rows with wrapped descriptions match Chrome.
- **F19 done** in the fulgur fork (`2026-10-16-f19-row-backgrounds.md`): `<tr>` and row-group backgrounds paint
  behind their cells, also on repeated headers; 03's total row is a dark bar with visible white text, 03 8.2 → 6.2 %.
- **F12 done** in the fulgur fork (`2026-10-16-f12-repeat-thead.md`): the first `table-header-group` repeats on every
  continuation page (header ≤ ¼ page), copy painted as an artifact. 02 worst page 11.8 → 11.5 %. Open as **F12b**:
  `tfoot` repetition.
- **F11 done** in the fulgur and Blitz forks (`2026-10-15-f11-none-colour-components.md`): `none` colour components
  behave as 0, as in Chrome.
- **F14 done** in the Blitz fork (`2026-10-15-f14-calc-cell-width.md`): a mixed `calc()` cell width is `auto`, no
  panic; matches Chrome on all probes.
- **F5 done** in the Blitz fork (`2026-10-15-f5-table-column-widths.md` §5): explicit cell widths no longer get the
  padding added under `border-box`; 02 paginates 3/3 like Chrome, 03 8.9 → 8.2 %. 02's threshold re-baselined per
  ADR-0003. Remaining 02 drift is F12 and F13 and Blitz #386.
- **F2b parked**: needs a fork (`stylo_static_prefs` or a Blitz bump of Stylo), which agent sessions cannot create;
  the F2 polyfill covers bundled CSS. Same root cause as F8 (a static pref missing from `stylo_static_prefs`).
- **F8 done** (polyfill, see `2026-10-15-f8-gradient-interpolation.md`): `in <colorspace>` is removed from gradients and
  custom-property values; gradients render. Root fix (enable the pref) waits with F2b.
- **F3b done** (local mapping, see `2026-10-15-f3b-generic-families.md`): generic keywords are rewritten to the bundled
  family they map to (`monospace` → built-in mono, others → first bundled font, `generic_family` overrides).
- **F7 done** (polyfill, see `2026-10-15-f7-print-media.md`): `print`/`screen` media types are swapped in bundled CSS
  and `<style>` elements, so queries evaluate as on a print device. Root fix (Blitz device media type) possible in the Blitz fork.
- F1 has a second surface: `usvg` (inline SVG) cannot parse `oklch()` fills either ("Fallback to black").

## Order of work

1. ~~F3~~ → 2. ~~F2~~ (prelude; borders/shadows) → 3. ~~F1~~ (colours, incl. SVG) → 4. ~~F7~~ (print media)
→ 5. ~~F3b~~ (generic families) → 6. F2b (enable Stylo `@property`; parked — fork) → 7. ~~F8~~ (gradient interpolation)
→ 8. ~~F4~~ → 9. ~~F5~~ → 10. ~~F10~~ (panic on malformed CSS) → ~~F19~~ (`<tr>` backgrounds — 03 total invisible) → 11. ~~F14~~ (`calc(%)` cell width panic — a crash
on valid CSS goes first) → 12. ~~F11~~ (`none` colour components) → 13. ~~F12~~ (repeat `<thead>`) → 14. ~~F13~~ (cell
`vertical-align: middle`) → 15. F15 (table algorithm gaps)
→ 16. F17 (SVG presentation colours) → 17. F18 (box-shadow blur, outline, SVG opacity) → 18. F16 (`none` in mixes)
→ 19. F23 (background-image 4/3) → 20. F24 (w-full table overflow) → 21. F25 (data: SVG backgrounds).
